description: manic_episode is present for the majority of the total duration of the active and residual portions of the illness\n  manifestation: For most of this whole period, I’ve been cycling back Yeah, I have. Um... there have been times, like whole weeks where I felt completely calm. I wasn't revved up, I was sleeping normally, just going about my regular day.
